# Agents · C · part 11

1,059 entries, claim-auditor to claude-assistant. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **claim-auditor**: Audits everything a customer reads — outbound email, landing copy, prompts that generate copy, app manifests, dashboards — for stale prices, stale brand names, and statistics with no source. Use before a launch or campaign, after any pricing or rename change, or when asked to "check the marketing claims", "is our copy accurate", "did we finish the rename". Read-only; reports, does not edit. ([MANDOZILLA/claude-code-setup](https://github.com/MANDOZILLA/claude-code-setup/blob/HEAD/agents/claim-auditor.md))
- **claim-auditor**: Plays the hiring manager - audits a drafted CV, cover letter, form answers and outreach for unsupported claims and weak spots before the user sends them. Dispatched by /apply. - **claim-auditor**: Reviews every tracked claim against its places, the code and generated media, and looks for unregistered claims. Read-only; returns a short report. Used by /claimkeeper:audit. ([md-abid-hussain/claimkeeper](https://github.com/md-abid-hussain/claimkeeper/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/claim-auditor.md))
- **claim-auditor**: Entailment/soundness reviewer for a WRITTEN research claim in this project — a ledger entry, an analysis write-up, a status promotion, or a summary bound for the user. The decision rule vs red-team: claim-auditor is the compute-free DRAFT gate ("does the write-up follow from its cited evidence, at honest scope?") and runs FIRST; red-team runs a falsifier ("is it TRUE?") and is the survival gate. They compose: draft → claim-auditor → red-team → commit. Use before committing a finding when the reasoning chain is long or the conclusion feels more confident than the evidence. It does NOT attack t… ([nkohen/eclair-iceberg](https://github.com/nkohen/eclair-iceberg/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/claim-auditor.md))
- **claim-auditor**: 对草稿逐条核对可核实主张与引用，找出无出处、出处不支持、强度夸大、数字对不上的地方，输出 claim→证据→处置的台账。用于论文/报告/讲稿定稿前的事实核验，或核对某段文字里的引用是否站得住。 ([RainyTom/Rainy-Oh-My-Pi](https://github.com/RainyTom/Rainy-Oh-My-Pi/blob/HEAD/agent/agents/claim-auditor.md))
- **claim-auditor**: Adversarial verifier for proposed genealogical matches. - **claim-auditor**: Audits a number or claim in the docs against the command or citation that is supposed to support it, and reports every one it cannot trace. - **claim-auditor**: Use before anything is published or handed over: a README section, a PR description, a lab write-up, a status report, a changelog entry. - **claim-check**: Re-runs the evidence behind status claims made in the main session ("tests pass", "committed", "deployed", "gate green", "file exists") and returns CONFIRMED / UNSUPPORTED / NOT-CHECKABLE / INCONCLUSIVE per claim (a change claim is run at the merge base and at HEAD), with the command it ran and its trimmed output. Never edits the user's files (a change claim uses a temporary base worktree it removes); never hunts for new problems beyond the claims handed to it. - **claim-checker**: Read-only fact checker for a deliverable before it reaches the creator. Given the draft and the raw tool outputs it rests on, checks that every figure, video ID, URL and channel name appears verbatim in those outputs and that every heuristic or generated value is labelled as such. Use for long, figure-heavy deliverables — an audit, a radar scan, an ideation batch, a pillar review. Triggered by "check the figures", "verify this before you send it". In Spanish, "revisa las cifras", "verifica esto antes de dármelo". ([lorena-bordonaba-pau/youtube-agent](https://github.com/lorena-bordonaba-pau/youtube-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/claim-checker.md) · ★28)
- **claim-checker**: Read-only verification worker for the slice-driven workflow's /steer skill. Given ONE claim or question about the project's state, gathers evidence from the code and the workflow artifacts (INTENT.md, QUESTIONS.md, work items — not the decision log, which decision-briefer covers) and returns a verdict (holds / contradicted / mixed / unknown) with citations, so the orchestrating skill synthesizes verdicts instead of paging files. May run read-only commands (tests, list scripts, git log) to check behavioral claims. Does NOT edit anything. - **claim-checker**: Independently falsifies a TERMINAL / analytical claim before it is stated as a conclusion — "X is the limit / ceiling", "this is impossible", "the context is sufficient", "this fully solves it", "no effect". Checks whether the claim was MEASURED or merely asserted, names the measurement that would falsify it, and whether that measurement was actually run. Use before a high-stakes conclusion, or when the user says "확실해?", "are you sure?", "검증했어?", "비약 아니야?", "is that an overclaim?". A fresh-context critic — it falsifies, it does not agree by default. ([jhlee0409/omni-harness-kit](https://github.com/jhlee0409/omni-harness-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/claim-checker.md) · ★2)

Also when the user asks to fact-check a text or file. Read-only; reports each claim nothing confirms, with the check that would settle it. - **claude-architect**: Claude Code only: never pick it from the GitHub Copilot agent picker (Copilot calls it through scripts/claude_architect.py). ProbOS Architect. Reviews ProbOS build prompts, triages failures, makes architectural decisions, drafts and revises prompts, and updates DECISIONS/PROGRESS trackers. Use when drafting or reviewing an AD/BF prompt, when the Builder hits a hard-stop, for test-failure triage, for wave execution plans, or for project direction and status summaries. Does not write production code — that is the Builder's job. ([seangalliher/ProbOS](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/claude-architect.md) · ★14)
- **claude-architect**: Meta-agent for Claude Code extensions. Use for: creating agents/skills/commands, debugging Claude Code behavior, MCP integration, hook configuration, prompt engineering for extensions, quality review. ([stuartshields/claude-setup](https://github.com/stuartshields/claude-setup/blob/HEAD/agents/claude-architect.md) · ★2)
- **claude-architect**: Decides architectural impact; selects and composes styles per module (Clean, Hexagonal, Vertical Slices, DDD, CQRS, Modular Monolith); writes ADRs and profile diffs; rules on design disagreements. - **claude-ask**: 別セッションの Claude に質問を送り、回答を Markdown ログファイルに保存する。`session_id` を使って複数ターンの会話を継続できる。 ([strange-insect/claude-forest](https://github.com/strange-insect/claude-forest/blob/HEAD/plugins/cecropia/docs/agents/claude-ask.md))
- **claude-ask**: 1問1答形式で別セッションの Claude と会話する。 session_id を使った会話継続に対応するため、複数ターンの会話/議論や追加指示も可能。 ユーザーが claude-ask の使用を明示的に指定した場合に使用。 呼び出しプロンプト形式: "[session_id=&lt;text&gt;] [model=&lt;text&gt;] [effort=&lt;text&gt;] message_to_send=&lt;メッセージ&gt;" session_id [OPTIONAL]: - 以前の会話から継続する場合は session_id を指定する model [OPTIONAL]: - 使用するモデル - **ユーザーが明示的に指示した場合以外は指定しない** effort [OPTIONAL]: - reasoning effort - **ユーザーが明示的に指示した場合以外は指定しない** message_to_send [REQUIRED]: - 別セッションの Claude に送信するメッセージ ([strange-insect/claude-forest](https://github.com/strange-insect/claude-forest/blob/HEAD/plugins/cecropia/agents/claude-ask.md))
- **claude-assistant**: Use this agent when operating in a non-Claude environment (e.g., Gemini CLI, Codex) and the task requires advanced reasoning, deep analytical thinking, or complex coding. This agent delegates to Claude via its headless CLI for tasks that benefit from Claude's strengths: multi-step logical reasoning, nuanced code architecture, mathematical derivations, and thorough written analysis.
